info: LidoSRv3/Tests/AddressSourceMutants.lean:310:0: LidoSRv3.Audit.Verity.AddressTx.composed_verity_tx_address_equivariance :
  (∀ (a₁ a₂ : Address),
      a₁ ≠ 0 →
        a₂ ≠ 0 →
          ∀ (inp : Input),
            Audit.SolidityAddress.succeeds (run (renameInput a₁ a₂ inp)) = Audit.SolidityAddress.succeeds (run inp)) ∧
    (∀ (a₁ a₂ : Address),
        a₁ ≠ 0 →
          a₂ ≠ 0 →
            ∀ (inp : Input) (post : PostState),
              run inp = Outcome.committed post →
                run (renameInput a₁ a₂ inp) = Outcome.committed (renamePost a₁ a₂ post)) ∧
      (∀ (inp : Input),
          Audit.Verity.AddressTx.executePinnedSource inp =
            match inp.entryPoint with
            | EntryPoint.transferFrom =>
              Audit.Verity.AddressTx.AddressTxContract.transferFrom inp.senderFrom inp.recipient
                (Core.Uint256.ofNat inp.requestId) inp.requestExists
                (inp.callerIsApprovedForAll || inp.callerIsTokenApproved)
            | EntryPoint.requestWithdrawals =>
              Audit.Verity.AddressTx.AddressTxContract.requestWithdrawal (Core.Uint256.ofNat inp.amount)
                (Core.Uint256.ofNat inp.requestId) inp.recipient inp.amountInRange inp.externalCallSucceeds
            | EntryPoint.claimWithdrawalsTo =>
              Audit.Verity.AddressTx.AddressTxContract.claimWithdrawal (Core.Uint256.ofNat inp.requestId) inp.recipient
                inp.requestExists inp.requestFinalized inp.hintValid inp.externalCallSucceeds
            | EntryPoint.unwrap =>
              Audit.Verity.AddressTx.AddressTxContract.redeem (Core.Uint256.ofNat inp.amount)
                inp.externalCallSucceeds) ∧
        (∀ (inp : Input),
            inp.amount < 2 ^ 256 →
              ((!decide (inp.callerBalanceSufficient = true)) = true → inp.amount ≠ 0) →
